       @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap');
       *{font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;}
       input, textarea, select { -webkit-appearance: none; appearance: none; font-size: 16px !important; }
       button { min-height: 44px; -webkit-appearance: none; touch-action: none; }
       canvas { touch-action: none; -webkit-touch-callout: none; -webkit-user-select: none; user-select: none; }
       .no-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
       .no-scrollbar{ -ms-overflow-style:none; scrollbar-width:none}
       button:active { background: #7A1F1F !important; color: #fff !important; border: 2px solid #000 !important; }
       @keyframes forwardMotion {
         0% { transform: scale(0.85); opacity: 0; }
         60% { opacity: 1; }
         100% { transform: scale(1.05); opacity: 1; }
       }
       #splash {
         perspective: 1200px;
         overflow: hidden;
       }
       .intro-splash-inner {
         display: flex;
         flex-direction: column;
         align-items: center;
         justify-content: center;
         width: 100%;
         height: 100%;
         padding: 24px;
         box-sizing: border-box;
         gap: 28px;
       }
       .intro-logos {
         display: flex;
         flex-wrap: wrap;
         justify-content: center;
         align-items: center;
         gap: 30px;
         width: 100%;
         max-width: 1200px;
       }
       .intro-logo-box {
         display: grid;
         place-items: center;
         will-change: transform, opacity;
         animation: forwardMotion 2.8s ease-out forwards;
         flex-shrink: 0;
         background: transparent;
       }
       .intro-logo-img {
         object-fit: contain;
         display: block;
         width: 100%;
         height: 100%;
       }
       .intro-logo-dg-box {
         width: 400px;
         height: 400px;
       }
       .intro-logo-dg-img {
         width: 400px;
         height: 400px;
       }
       .intro-logo-jm-box {
         width: 600px;
         height: 600px;
         animation-delay: 0.15s;
       }
       .intro-logo-jm-img {
         width: 600px;
         height: 600px;
       }
       .intro-progress-wrap {
         width: 80%;
         max-width: 520px;
         height: 4px;
         background: #333;
         border-radius: 999px;
         overflow: hidden;
       }
       .intro-progress-bar {
         height: 100%;
         background: #7A1F1F;
         border-radius: 999px;
         transition: width 0.08s linear;
       }
       @media (max-width: 768px) {
         .intro-logos {
           gap: 30px;
           justify-content: center;
           align-items: center;
         }
         .intro-logo-dg-box {
           width: 220px;
           height: 220px;
         }
         .intro-logo-dg-img {
           width: 220px;
           height: 220px;
         }
         .intro-logo-jm-box {
           width: 320px;
           height: 320px;
         }
         .intro-logo-jm-img {
           width: 320px;
           height: 320px;
         }
         .intro-progress-wrap {
           width: 80%;
         }
       }
